. In the next lemma, we aim to derive the time threshold which can fulfill this assumption.

Lemma C.8
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, given
\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}
,
for any
\theta\in(0,1)
, taking

t\leq\frac{\ln\left(\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}\,,

then
Eq.

24
holds with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-N)
for a universal constant
C
over random Gaussian data, i.e.

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{U}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}}\bm{A}_{0}+\bm{E}_{t})\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\theta\,.

Remark:
To ensure that the
\theta
-alignment phase still falls into the early phase in
Lemma

C.6
for
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{op}\leq\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}
, we need to choose proper initialization for
\bm{A}_{0}
.
We will detail this in
Theorem

3.2
later.

Proof.

First,
\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})
in
Lemma

C.5
can be lower bounded by

\begin{split}\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})&=\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}\left((\bm{I}_{d}+\eta\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}})^{t}+(\bm{I}_{d}-\eta\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}})^{t}\right)\\
&\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}\left((\bm{I}_{d}+\eta\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}})^{t}\right)\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)^{t}\,.\end{split}

(25)

Recall
Lemma

C.5
, we have
\lambda_{r^{*}+1}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})=1
and
Lemma

C.6
with
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{op}\leq\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}
, we define the following threshold
\gamma
and upper bound it

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle:=\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}+1}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}+\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)^{t}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\quad

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)\cdot t\right)\cdot\frac{4\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\,.

(26)

Set
\theta\in(0,1)
, let Eq.(
26
)
\leq\frac{\theta}{2}
, then we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{U}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}}\bm{A}_{0}+\bm{E}_{t})\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\theta\,.

The time
t
to achieve this angle
\theta
can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)\cdot t\right)\cdot\frac{4\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\leq\frac{\theta}{2}\,,

which implies that

\displaystyle t\leq\frac{\ln\left(\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}\,.

Finally we conclude the proof.
∎

Theorem C.9
.

[Full version of
Theorem

3.2
]
Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, recall
{\bm{G}}^{\natural}
defined in
Eq.

5
with its condition number
\kappa^{\natural}
, we consider random Gaussian initialization
\bm{A}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
with
[\bm{A}_{0}]_{ij}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\alpha^{2})
in (
LoRA-init
), for any
\theta\in(0,1)
, let
\xi=o(1)
be chosen such that

\alpha\leq\begin{cases}\left(\frac{\theta\xi}{24r\sqrt{d}}\right)^{\frac{3\kappa^{\natural}}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{27d}}&\text{if }r^{*}\leq r<2r^{*},\\
\left(\frac{\theta}{24\sqrt{d}}\right)^{\frac{3\kappa^{\natural}}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{27d}}&\text{if }r\geq 2r^{*}\,.\end{cases}

Then if we run gradient descent for
t^{*}
steps with

\displaystyle t^{*}\lesssim\begin{cases}\frac{\ln\left(\frac{24r\sqrt{d}}{\theta\xi}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}&\text{if }r^{*}\leq r<2r^{*},\\
\frac{\ln\left(\frac{24\sqrt{d}}{\theta}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}&\text{if }r\geq 2r^{*}\,,\end{cases}

we have the following alignment on the left singular subspace between
\bm{G}^{\natural}
and
\bm{A}_{t^{*}}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}(\bm{G}^{\natural})\bm{U}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t^{*}}\right)\right\|_{op}\lesssim\theta\,,

\displaystyle\mbox{with probability at least}~\begin{cases}1\!-\!C_{1}\exp(-d)\!-\!(C_{2}\xi)^{r-r^{*}+1}\!-\!C_{3}\exp(-r)\!-\!C\exp(-N)&\text{if }r^{*}\leq r<2r^{*},\\
1\!-\!C_{4}\exp(-d)-C_{5}\exp(-r)-C\exp(-N)&\text{if }r\geq 2r^{*}\,,\end{cases}

for some positive constants
C\,,C_{1}\,,C_{2}\,,C_{3}\,,C_{4}\,,C_{5}
.
Here
\bm{U}_{r^{*}}(\bm{A}_{t^{*}})
denotes the left singular subspace spanned by the
r^{*}
largest singular values of
\bm{A}_{t^{*}}
and
\bm{U}_{r^{*},\perp}(\bm{M})
denotes the left singular subspace orthogonal to
\bm{U}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{M}\right)
.Note that we can select any pair of stepsizes
(\eta\,,\eta)
that satisfies the conditions
t^{*}>1
,
\eta\geq\eta
, and
\zeta(\eta,\eta)=\Theta(1)
.

Proof.

For ease of description, we denote
\bm{A}_{0}:=\alpha\bm{T}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
where
\bm{T}
is a standard random Gaussian matrix with zero-mean and unit variance.
Here we aim to choose a proper
\alpha
to ensure that
\theta
-alignment phase in
Lemma

C.8
still falls into the early phase in
Lemma

C.6
, i.e.

\displaystyle\frac{\ln\left(\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}=\frac{\ln\left(\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{3\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}^{2}}\right)}{3\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})\right)}=t^{*}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\ln\left(\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\right)=\frac{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}{3\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})\right)}\ln\left(\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{3\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}=\left(\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{3\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}{3\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})\right)}}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\theta=\frac{8\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A}_{0})}\left(\frac{3\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\right)^{\frac{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}{3\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})\right)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{3\|\bm{A}_{0}\|_{op}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\right)^{\iota}

\left[\text{by setting }\iota:=\frac{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}{3\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})\right)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{8\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{3\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\right)^{\iota}\alpha^{2\iota}\,.

In the next, we will discuss how to pick up
\alpha
.
According to
Lemma

E.3
, we need to consider the following two cases on the relationship between
r^{*}
and
r
.

Case 1.

r^{*}\leq r<2r^{*}
: by
Lemma

E.2
and
Lemma

E.3
, with probability at least
1-C_{1}\exp(-d)-(C_{2}\xi)^{r-r^{*}+1}-C_{3}\exp(-r)
for some positive constants
C_{1}\,,C_{2}\,,C_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{3\sqrt{d}}\leq 1\,,\quad\frac{\xi}{r\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\lesssim 1\,.

(27)

Here we pick

\displaystyle\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\theta\xi}{24r\sqrt{d}}\right)^{\frac{3\kappa^{\natural}}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{27d}}\,,

then recall
Lemma

C.8
on the alignment, we take
\alpha
here

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}\left(-\nabla_{\bm{W}}\widetilde{L}(\bm{W}^{\natural})\right)\bm{U}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t^{*}}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{3\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\right)^{\iota}\alpha^{2\iota}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{8\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{3\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\right)^{\iota}\left(\frac{\theta\xi}{24r\sqrt{d}}\right)^{3\kappa^{\natural}\iota}\left(\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{27d}\right)^{\iota}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{8\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{9d}\right)^{\iota}\left(\frac{\theta\xi}{24r\sqrt{d}}\right)^{3\kappa^{\natural}\iota}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}\theta\xi}{3r\sqrt{d}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{9d}\right)^{\iota}\,.\quad

\left[\text{since }\iota\geq 1/3\kappa^{\natural}\text{ and }\frac{\theta\xi}{24r\sqrt{d}}\in(0,1)\right]

Then using
Eq.

27
, with probability at least
1-C_{1}\exp(-d)-(C_{2}\xi)^{r-r^{*}+1}-C_{3}\exp(-r)
for some positive constants
C_{1}\,,C_{2}\,,C_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}\left(-\nabla_{\bm{W}}\widetilde{L}(\bm{W}^{\natural})\right)\bm{U}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t^{*}}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\,.

And we can compute the upper bound of
t^{*}
as

\displaystyle t^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{\ln\left(\frac{8\|\bm{A}\|_{op}}{\theta\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{A})}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}\lesssim\frac{\ln\left(\frac{24r\sqrt{d}}{\theta\xi}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}\,.

Case 2.

r\geq 2r^{*}
: by
Lemma

E.2
and
Lemma

E.3
, with probability at least
1-C_{4}\exp(-d)-C_{5}\exp(-r)
for some positive constants
C_{4}\,,C_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}}{3\sqrt{d}}\leq 1\,,\quad\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\lesssim 1\,.

Here we pick

\displaystyle\alpha

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\theta}{24\sqrt{d}}\right)^{\frac{3\kappa^{\natural}}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}{27d}}\,.

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}\left(-\nabla_{\bm{W}}\widetilde{L}(\bm{W}^{\natural})\right)\bm{U}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}\theta}{3\sqrt{d}\lambda_{\min}(\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{r^{*}}(\bm{P}_{t}^{\bm{A}})\bm{T})}\left(\frac{\|\bm{T}\|_{op}^{2}}{9d}\right)^{\iota}\lesssim\theta\,.

And we can compute the upper bound of
t^{*}
as

\displaystyle t^{*}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ln\left(\frac{24\sqrt{d}}{\theta}\right)}{\ln\left(1+\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)\right)}\,.

∎

Theorem C.10
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, using the LoRA initialization for
\bm{B}_{0}=\bm{0}
, then for any time-step
t\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{V}^{\!\top}_{r^{*},\perp}\left(-\nabla_{\bm{W}}\widetilde{L}(\bm{W}^{\natural})\right)\bm{V}_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=0\,.

Proof.

We prove by induction. Recall the complete SVD of
\Delta
in
Eq.

1
as

\displaystyle\Delta=\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{S}}^{*}\widetilde{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}&\bm{U}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{S}^{*}&\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times(d-r^{*})}\\
\bm{0}_{(d-r^{*})\times r^{*}}&\bm{0}_{(d-r^{*})\times(d-r^{*})}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{V}^{\!\top}\\
\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\end{bmatrix}\,.

For
t=1
, recall
{\bm{G}}^{\natural}=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}\Delta
in
Eq.

5
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{1}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta}{N}\bm{A}_{0}^{\!\top}{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\frac{\eta}{N}\bm{A}_{0}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}\Delta\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{r\times(d-r^{*})}\,.

Assume
\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{r\times(d-r^{*})}
holds for any
t\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
t\geq 2
, then

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\frac{\eta}{N}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}+\frac{\eta}{N}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{r\times(d-r^{*})}\,,

which completes the claim.
∎

C.2
Gradient Descent under Spectral Initialization

For notational simplicity, we denote
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}:=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}
in the following content. Recall the negative gradient of Full Fine-tuning at the first step in
Eq.

5
, we write it here again

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}^{\natural}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{\bm{W}}\widetilde{L}(\bm{W}^{\natural})=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{\Delta}=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\Delta=\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\widetilde{\bm{V}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\,.

(28)

In this section, according to
Lemma

E.1
, the following statement

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right\|_{op}=\epsilon\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2\kappa}\,,\frac{c}{\kappa^{3}}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{2}\,,\quad\mbox{for some small constant $c$}\,,

(29)

holds with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
. We propose the following initialization scheme (
Spectral-init
)

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{0}=\left[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\right]_{[:,1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\,,\quad\bm{B}_{0}=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{V}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\right]_{[:,1:r]}^{\!\top}\,.

First, we have the following lemma.

Lemma C.11
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), recall
\kappa:=\lambda_{1}^{*}(\Delta)/\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}(\Delta)
, then with probability at least with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\|\Delta\|_{op}\leq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\,,

(30)

and

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}}{2}\,,\quad\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}}{2}\,.

(31)

Proof.

Due to
\operatorname{rank}\left({\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right)=r^{*}
and
r\geq r^{*}
, then
\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}={\bm{G}}^{\natural}
. Accordingly, by
Eq.

29
, with probability at least
1-2\exp(-c\epsilon^{2}N)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\right\|_{op}+\left\|{\bm{G}}^{\natural}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{G}}^{\natural}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\Delta\right\|_{op}

[using
Eq.

28
]

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right\|_{op}\left\|\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\|\Delta\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\kappa}\left\|\Delta\right\|_{op}

[using
Eq.

29
]

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\,.

Then, using the above result and Weyl’s inequality, we have the upper bound
\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\right)\leq\lambda_{1}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{0}\right)
and the lower bound

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\right)=\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)\geq\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\Delta\right)-\left\|{\bm{G}}^{\natural}-\Delta\right\|_{op}=\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\Delta\right)-\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}\geq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\,.

Now we are ready to give the lower bound of
\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{0}\right)
.
Because of
\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}=\bm{G}^{\natural}
under spectral initialization, we have

\lambda_{1}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{1}({\bm{G}}^{\natural})}\leq\sqrt{\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right\|_{op}\lambda_{1}(\Delta)}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon\lambda_{1}(\Delta)}\,,\quad\mbox{with high probability at least}~1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)\,.

where we use
\bm{G}^{\natural}=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\Delta
and the concentration results on
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}
. Then combining the above two inequalities,
\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{0}\right)
is lower bounded by

\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{B}_{0}\right)\geq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\right)}{\lambda_{1}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)}\geq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}/2}{\lambda_{1}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)}\geq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}}{2}\,,

by taking
\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{2\kappa}
.
The lower bound of
\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\right)
can be obtained similarly.
∎

The following lemma indicates
\bm{B}_{t}
’s GD dynamics stay in the low-dimensional target subspace under the spectral initialization.

Lemma C.12
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), during the iteration, for any
t\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, we always have
\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}
, where
\bm{V}_{\perp}
comes from the complete SVD of
\Delta
in
Eq.

1
.

Proof.

We prove it by induction.
First, recall the SVD of
\Delta
in
Eq.

1
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\Delta\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}\,,

and

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{0}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{V}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\right]_{[:,1:r]}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{-1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\right]_{[:,1:r]}{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{-1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\right]_{[:,1:r]}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\Delta\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}\,.

Next, We prove by induction. Starting from
t=1
, using the above two equations, we have

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{1}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{0}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\frac{\eta_{2}}{N}\bm{A}^{\!\top}_{0}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\Bigl(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0})-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}\Bigr)\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{0}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\frac{\eta_{2}}{N}\bm{A}^{\!\top}_{0}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\bm{V}_{\perp}+\eta\bm{A}^{\!\top}_{0}{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}\,.

Assume
\bm{B}_{t}{\bm{V}_{\perp}}=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}
holds for any
t=2,3,\cdots
, then at
t+1
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\frac{\eta}{N}\bm{A}^{\!\top}_{t}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}+\eta_{2}\bm{A}^{\!\top}_{t}{\bm{G}}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}\,.

Accordingly we finish the proof.
∎

Under spectral initialization, we have already demonstrated that
\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}
is close to
\Delta
. In the following content, we aim to track how
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{op}
behaves (in a local sense), which is a critical ingredient to study both the loss and risk of LoRA training. In this regime, there is no significant difference on setting different step-size
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
. For ease of description, we set
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}:=\eta
.

Here we can characterize the operator norm of
\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)
as

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bigg(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\bigg)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{V}&\bm{V}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{op}\quad

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\bm{U}\bm{S}^{*}\right\|_{op}\quad

\displaystyle=\left\|\bigg(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\bigg)\bigg(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\bm{U}\bm{S}^{*}\bigg)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{U}\bigg(\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\bm{S}^{*}\bigg)\right\|_{op}+\left\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\bm{S}^{*}\right\|_{op}}_{\mbox{signal space}}+\underbrace{\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right\|_{op}}_{\mbox{complementary}}\,,

(32)

where the first term denotes the loss in the signal space
\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}\bm{B}\bm{V}-\bm{S}^{*}\right\|_{op}
and the second term denotes the complementary space decay
\left\|\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\bm{A}\bm{B}\bm{V}\right\|_{op}
. Next, we need a new parametrization to track the dynamics of these two terms. Recall the complete SVD of
\Delta
in
Eq.

1
as

\displaystyle\Delta=\widetilde{\bm{U}}\widetilde{\bm{S}}^{*}\widetilde{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}&\bm{U}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{S}^{*}&\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times(d-r^{*})}\\
\bm{0}_{(d-r^{*})\times r^{*}}&\bm{0}_{(d-r^{*})\times(d-r^{*})}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{V}^{\!\top}\\
\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\end{bmatrix}\,.

For notational simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}:=\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\,,\quad\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}:=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\,,\quad\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}:=\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\,,\quad\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}:=\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}_{\perp}}\,.

and thus

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{t}:=(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}\,,\quad\bm{R}_{t}^{*}:=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}-\bm{S}^{*}\,,\quad\bm{R}_{t}^{\perp}:=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\,.

Accordingly,
Eq.

32
can be reformulated as
\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq\left\|\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\right\|_{op}+\left\|\bm{R}_{t}^{\perp}\right\|_{op}
. By
Lemma

C.12
, we have
\bm{B}^{\bm{V}_{\perp}}=\bm{0}_{r\times(k-r^{*})}

\forall\,t\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
.
Next, we can track
\bm{R}^{*}_{t}
and
\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}
via the following two lemmas.

Lemma C.13
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), we have the following reparametrized iterates

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\,,

(33)

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\,,

(34)

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}-\eta\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}

\displaystyle-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\,.

(35)

Proof.

Recall the gradient update for
\bm{A}_{t+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\,.

Recall
\bm{R}_{t}:=(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}
and
\Delta=\bm{U}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{V}^{\!\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\!\top}+\bm{V}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\!\top}+\bm{V}_{\perp}\bm{V}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\Delta\bm{V}\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\Delta\bm{V}\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right)^{\!\top}\quad

[by
Lemma

C.12
]

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{U}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\bm{S}^{*}\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}\right)^{\!\top}\,.

Accordingly, the recursion for
\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t+1}
is reformulated as

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\,.

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\,.

Regarding the recursion for
\bm{B}_{t+1}
, we can derive in a similar way

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{V}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\bm{V}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\bm{V}

\displaystyle-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\bm{V}\,,

which implies

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}-\eta\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\,.

∎

In the next, we are able to characterize the upper bound of
\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}
.

Lemma C.14
.

Denote
\mathcal{M}_{t}:=\max\left\{\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\right\}
,
under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), then we choose
\epsilon
with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}\,,

and

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{\min}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

(36)

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}\,.

Proof.

Here we first track the dynamics of
\bm{R}^{*}_{t}
. We have

\displaystyle\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}}-\bm{S}^{*}

\displaystyle=\bm{R}^{*}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}-\eta\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}

\displaystyle------

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle------

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\,.

Then, we take operator norm over the above equation. Hence, with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\,.

Next, we take maximum over
\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}
and
\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}
on the right hand side above. Recall
\mathcal{M}_{t}=\max\left\{\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\right\}
, using the fact that
\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\mathcal{M}_{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}\,.

Next, we track the dynamics of
\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}
. We have

\displaystyle\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}}

\displaystyle=\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}-\eta\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{*}_{t}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle-\eta\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)^{\!\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\,.

Then, we take operator norm over the above equation. With probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{\min}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\,.

Next, we take maximum over
\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}
and
\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}
on the right hand side above. Recall
\mathcal{M}_{t}=\max\left\{\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t}\right\|_{op}\right\}
, using the fact that
\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\mathcal{M}_{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\left(\lambda_{\min}^{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}\right)+\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta^{2}\epsilon\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}^{\bm{V}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\mathcal{M}_{t}^{2}\,.

Finally we conclude the proof.
∎

Before we move to the main proof, we need to establish a strict upper bound on
\bm{A}_{t}
and
\bm{B}_{t}
.

Lemma C.15
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, suppose
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq\lambda_{1}^{*}
and
\eta\leq\frac{1}{10\lambda_{1}^{*}}
, if
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
and
\left\|\bm{B}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
, we choose
\epsilon
satisfying
Eq.

29
, then with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,\quad\left\|\bm{B}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,.

Proof.

Inspired by
Soltanolkotabi et al. (2023)
, we recall the stacked iterate
\bm{Z}_{t}
defined in
Eq.

12
and construct an anti-iterate

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}:=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\\
-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\end{bmatrix}\,.

Additionally, we define a perturbation matrix

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}_{d\times d}&\left(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\\
\bm{R}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)&\bm{0}_{k\times k}\end{bmatrix}\,.

Then, we can reformulate the recursion of
\bm{Z}_{t+1}
as

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{Z}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}-\bm{\Gamma}\right)\bm{Z}_{t}+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{I}_{2d}-\eta\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{Z}_{t}+\eta\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{Z}_{t}-\eta\bm{\Gamma}\bm{Z}_{t}+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}\,,

where
\bm{\Gamma}
is defined as

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}

\displaystyle:=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}_{d\times d}&\Delta\\
\Delta^{\!\top}&\bm{0}_{k\times k}\end{bmatrix}\,.

Then, by the triangle inequality, with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{2d}-\eta\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\bm{\Gamma}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\quad

[by simultaneous diagonalization]

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\bm{\Gamma}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\left\|\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,

where the last inequality follows from the fact that

\displaystyle\left\|\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Gamma}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{1}^{*}\,,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\quad\mbox{w.h.p.}~1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)\,.

Using the assumption

\displaystyle\left\|\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{B}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq\lambda_{1}^{*}\,,

then
\left\|\bm{Z}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}
can be further bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}^{2}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\right)\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,.

(37)

Denote
x=\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}
and
f(x)=\left(1-\eta x^{2}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\right)x
, we have
f^{\prime}(x)=1+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}-3\eta x^{2}
and
f^{\prime\prime}(x)=-6\eta x
. Then, we know
f^{\prime}(x^{*})=0
for
x>0
attained at
x^{*}=\sqrt{\frac{1+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}}{3\eta}}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{3\eta}+\frac{2}{3}\lambda_{1}^{*}}
. As we pick
\eta\leq\frac{1}{10\lambda_{1}^{*}}
, then
x^{*}\geq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
, which implies the maximum of
f(x)
attained at
x^{*}=2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
over
x\in[0\,,2\lambda_{1}^{*}]
since
\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
and

\displaystyle f(2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}})=2(1-4\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*})\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}=2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}-4\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,

which directly implies
\left\|\bm{Z}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
. By consequence,
\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{B}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
if
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{B}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}
, since
\bm{A}_{t+1}
and
\bm{B}_{t+1}
are sub-matrices of
\bm{Z}_{t+1}
.
∎

Based on the above results, we are ready to present the intermediate results on
\mathcal{M}_{t}
,
\bm{A}_{t}
,
\bm{B}_{t}
, and
\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}
.

Lemma C.16
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), we take
\epsilon
in data concentration as

\displaystyle\epsilon\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2\kappa}\,,\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{32\kappa(32\lambda_{1}^{*}+128\kappa^{2})}\right\}\,,

and set the step-size as

\displaystyle\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{128\kappa\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,\frac{(1-\epsilon/\kappa)}{1152\lambda^{*}_{1}}\right\}\,,

then with probability at least with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have that
\forall\,t\geq 0

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{t}\leq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{2}

(38)

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\right\|_{op}\,,\left\|\bm{B}_{t}\right\|_{op}\right\}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,

(39)

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\right)\,,\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)\geq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}}{4\sqrt{\kappa}}\,,

(40)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}\leq\frac{32\kappa\epsilon\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}}{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}\,.

(41)

Also, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{t+1}\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{64\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\,.

(42)

Proof.

Inspired by the matrix sensing technique from
Xiong et al. (2024)
, we develop an inductive approach to prove the claims on our settings.
First, at
t=0
,
Eq.

38
-
Eq.

41
can be adopted from
Lemma

C.11
. Next, we assume
Eq.

38
-
Eq.

41
hold at
t\geq 1
, recall
Eq.

34
, by the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\lambda_{r^{*}}^{2}\left(\bm{B}_{t}^{\bm{V}}\right)\right)\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}+4\eta\epsilon\mathcal{M}_{t}\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{(\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}})^{2}}{16\kappa}\bigg)\left\|\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right\|_{op}+2\eta\epsilon\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{(\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}})^{2}}{16\kappa}\bigg)\frac{32\kappa\epsilon\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}}{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}+2\eta\epsilon\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32\kappa\epsilon\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}}{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}\,,

which proves the
Eq.

41
at
t+1
. Next, by
Lemma

C.14
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{*}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{8\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+8\eta\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+4\eta^{2}\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+64\eta\epsilon\kappa^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+32\eta^{2}\kappa^{2}\epsilon\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\mathcal{M}_{t}+128\eta^{2}\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+64\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+8\eta\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+8\eta^{2}\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+8\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}+128\eta\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}\mathcal{M}_{t}+64\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{8\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\Bigg\{16\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+\eta\epsilon\left(4\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+32\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+8\lambda_{1}^{*}\right)+128\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left(128\eta\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+8\eta\epsilon^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\right)+128\eta\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\Bigg\}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{8\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta\bigg(16\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\quad

[due to the order dominance]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{16\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta\bigg(16\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\quad

\left[\text{by }\eta\leq\frac{1}{64\kappa\lambda_{1}^{*}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{32\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\,,\quad

\left[\text{by }\epsilon\leq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{16\kappa(32\lambda_{1}^{*}+128\kappa^{2})}\right]

where the order dominance from the second inequality follows from the fact that
\eta
and
\epsilon
are sufficiently small constant such that the terms in
\mathcal{O}(\eta\epsilon)\,,\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{2})\,,\mathcal{O}(\eta^{2}\epsilon^{2})\,,\mathcal{O}(\eta\epsilon^{3})
are significantly smaller the terms in
\mathcal{O}(\eta)
and
\mathcal{O}(\epsilon)
.

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}^{\perp}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{16\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\quad

\left[\text{since }\lambda_{\min}\left(\bm{A}^{\bm{U}_{\perp}}_{t}\right)\geq 0\right]

\displaystyle+\eta\Bigg\{8\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+4\eta\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}+32\eta\epsilon\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+64\eta\epsilon\kappa^{2}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+128\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}

\displaystyle+8\eta\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+8\eta\epsilon^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+2048\epsilon^{3}\frac{\kappa^{3}}{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}+64\eta\epsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}+128\eta\epsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}\Bigg\}\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{16\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta\Bigg\{8\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+2\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}\Bigg\}\mathcal{M}_{t}\quad

[due to the order dominance]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{32\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}+2\eta\Bigg\{8\epsilon\lambda_{1}^{*}+64\epsilon\kappa^{2}\Bigg\}\mathcal{M}_{t}\quad

\left[\text{by }\eta\leq\frac{1}{128\kappa\lambda_{1}^{*}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{64\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\,,\quad

\left[\text{by }\epsilon\leq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{32\kappa(32\lambda_{1}^{*}+128\kappa^{2})}\right]

which proves the
Eq.

38
at
t+1
.

Therefore, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{64\kappa}\bigg)\mathcal{M}_{t}\,.

Next, assume
Eq.

38
-
Eq.

41
hold at
t\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t+1}-\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\right)-\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}-\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\eta^{2}\bm{B}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{A}_{t}\,.

Accordingly, we can derive

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t+1}-\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\right)-\left(\bm{A}_{0}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{0}-\bm{B}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{t+1}\left\|\left(\bm{A}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{i}-\bm{B}_{i}\bm{B}_{i}^{\!\top}\right)-\left(\bm{A}_{i-1}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{i-1}-\bm{B}_{i-1}\bm{B}_{i-1}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{t+1}2\eta^{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\|_{op}^{2}\|\bm{R}_{i-1}\|_{op}^{2}\max\left\{\|\bm{A}_{i-1}\|_{op}^{2}\,,\|\bm{B}_{i-1}\|_{op}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{t+1}72\eta^{2}\mathcal{M}_{i-1}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\quad

[by
Eq.

29
]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{t+1}18\eta^{2}\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{64\kappa}\bigg)^{2(i-1)}(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*})^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}

\displaystyle\leq 18\eta^{2}(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*})^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\bigg(1-\eta\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{64\kappa}\bigg)^{2i}

\displaystyle\leq 18\eta^{2}(\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*})^{2}\lambda_{1}^{*}\frac{64\kappa}{\eta\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle=1152\eta\lambda_{1}^{*}\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\kappa

\displaystyle\leq(1-\epsilon/\kappa)\lambda_{1}^{*}\,.\quad

\left[\text{by }\eta\leq\frac{(1-\epsilon/\kappa)}{1152\lambda^{*}_{1}}\right]

Since
\left\|\left(\bm{A}_{0}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{0}-\bm{B}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}=0
due to the spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), by triangle inequality,
\left\|\left(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t+1}-\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq(1-\epsilon/\kappa)\lambda_{1}^{*}
. Next, by
Lemma

C.15
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,\quad\left\|\bm{B}_{t+1}\right\|_{op}\leq 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,

which proves the
Eq.

39
at
t+1
. Lastly, assume
Eq.

38
-
Eq.

41
hold at
t\geq 1
, by Weyl’s inequality, combine with
\mathcal{M}_{t+1}\leq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\geq\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}}-\bm{S}^{*}\right\|_{op}\geq\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}-\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\Rightarrow\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\geq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\,.

Again by Weyl’s inequality and the
Eq.

39
at time
t+1
we can get

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\lambda_{1}^{*}}\cdot\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\geq\lambda_{1}(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}})\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\geq\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}}\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\geq\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{2}\Rightarrow\lambda_{r^{*}}(\bm{B}_{t+1}^{\bm{V}})\geq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}}{4\sqrt{\kappa}}\,.

Besides,
\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}(\bm{A}_{t+1}^{\bm{U}})
follows similar derivation. We prove all the claims.
∎

Theorem C.17
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), we take
\epsilon
in data concentration as

\displaystyle\epsilon\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2\kappa}\,,\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{32\kappa(32\lambda_{1}^{*}+128\kappa^{2})}\right\}\,,

and set the step-size as

\displaystyle\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{128\kappa\lambda_{1}^{*}}\,,\frac{(1-\epsilon/\kappa)}{1152\lambda^{*}_{1}}\right\}\,,

(43)

then with probability at least with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have that
\forall\,t\geq 0

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2r^{*}}\left(1-\eta\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{64\kappa}\right)^{t}\cdot\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\,.

Proof.

By
Lemma

C.16
, with probability at least with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we can obtain the linear convergence of generalization risk

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2r^{*}}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\left[\operatorname{Rank}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})=r^{*}\text{ by \lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{linear-invariant-B2} and }\operatorname{Rank}(\Delta)=r^{*}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2r^{*}}\left(1-\eta\frac{\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{64\kappa}\right)^{t}\cdot\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\,,

which is independent of the choice of LoRA rank
r
if
r\geq r^{*}
.
∎

Remark:
The above convergence rate is independent of the choice of LoRA rank
r
if
r\geq r^{*}
.
It achieves an
\varepsilon
-risk in
\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{3}\ln\left(1/\varepsilon\right)\right)
iterations. The linear convergence rate heavily depends on
\kappa
.

C.3
Preconditioned Gradient Descent under Spectral Initialization

The convergence rate in
Theorem

C.17
will become slow if the downstream feature shift
\Delta
is ill-conditioned (i.e.,
\kappa
is extremely large). This motivates us to add preconditioners, which is a key technique to accelerate convergence in matrix factorization/sensing
(
Tong et al., 2021
;
Zhang et al., 2021
;
Zhang et al., 2023
;
Jia et al., 2024
)
. The preconditioners are derived from a Riemannian metric in
Mishra et al. (2012)
which originally are formulated as
(\bm{A}\bm{A}^{\top})^{-1}
and
(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{B})^{-1}
. Also, there is an efficient variant, i.e.
(\bm{A}\bm{A}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I}_{r})^{-1}
and
(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{B}+\lambda\bm{I}_{r})^{-1}
, which commonly used in practice for numerical stability via the preconditioning parameter
\lambda\geq 0
.

In the over-ranked setting (
r>r^{*}
),
\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}
and
\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}
are not necessarily invertible. Hence we add the following preconditioners to vanilla GD (
4
)

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}-\frac{\eta}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\left(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}\right)\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\,,

(Prec-GD)

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}-\frac{\eta}{N}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\left(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}\right)\,,

where
\bm{M}^{\dagger}
denotes the pseudo-inverse of a matrix
\bm{M}
. Such modified preconditioners are also considered in
Li et al. (2025)
.

In the following proofs, we will prove that the LoRA fine-tuning can achieve faster linear convergence which is independent of condition number
\kappa
under (
Spectral-init
) and (
Prec-GD
). Similar to
Lemma

C.12
, the dynamics of
\bm{B}_{t}
are still limited to the
r^{*}
-dimensional singular subspace
\bm{V}
of
\Delta
under (
Spectral-init
). We can verify this fact by the following lemma.

Lemma C.18
.

For any natural number
t\geq 0
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with (
Spectral-init
) and (
Prec-GD
), we have

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{0}_{r\times(k-r^{*})}\,.

Proof.

For
t=0
, recall the SVD of
\mathbf{G}^{\natural}
, i.e.
\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}\widetilde{\bm{V}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}
in
Eq.

28
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{0}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{-1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\right]_{[:,1:r]}\mathbf{G}^{\natural}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\left[\widetilde{\bm{S}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{-1/2}\right]_{[1:r]}\left[\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\bm{G}^{\natural}}^{\!\top}\right]_{[:,1:r]}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\Delta\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{r\times(k-r^{*})}\,.

Assume
\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}=\bm{0}_{d\times(d-r^{*})}
holds for any natural number
t\geq 1
, then

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\frac{\eta}{N}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\left(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)-\widetilde{\bm{Y}}\right)\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}\bm{V}_{\perp}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\bm{V}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{0}_{r\times(k-r^{*})}\,,\quad

[by our inductive hypothesis]

which proves the claim.
∎

We can re-formulate (
Prec-GD
) to be

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}-\eta\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\,,

(44)

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}-\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\,.

(45)

Before we start our main proofs, we first define the following notations

•

SVD of product matrix
\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}:=\mathcal{U}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}^{\!\top}
, where
\mathcal{U}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r^{*}}
,
\mathcal{S}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r^{*}\times r^{*}}
, and
\mathcal{V}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times r^{*}}
. Notice that here we employ rank-
r^{*}
SVD of
\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
since
\operatorname{Rank}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)\leq r^{*}
due to
Lemma

C.18
and
\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{r}\right)>0
strictly which we will obtain from
Theorem

C.21
.

•

The left compact singular matrix of
\bm{A}_{t}
as
\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
.

•

The right compact singular matrix of
\bm{B}_{t}
as
\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times r^{*}}
. Notice that here we take the top-
r^{*}
right singular subspace of
\bm{B}_{t}
due to
Lemma

C.18
.

By the pseudo inverse theorem and
Jia et al. (2024, Lemma 14)
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}=\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\,,

(46)

\displaystyle\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{B}_{t}=\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\,.

(47)

\displaystyle\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}=\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}^{-1}_{t}\mathcal{U}_{t}^{\!\top}\,.

(48)

Lemma C.19
.

Denote
\bm{R}_{t}:=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta
,
\bm{\Xi}:=\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with (
Prec-GD
), then we have

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta^{2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}^{-1}_{t}\mathcal{U}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\,.

Proof.

With
Eq.

44
and
Eq.

45
, we can construct

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\bm{A}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{B}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{B}_{t}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{\dagger}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\quad

[by
Eq.

46
and
Eq.

47
]

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}^{-1}_{t}\mathcal{U}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\quad

[by
Eq.

48
]

\displaystyle=\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta^{2}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}^{-1}_{t}\mathcal{U}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\,,

which proves the claim.
∎

In the next, we aim to estimate the signal part
\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}
.

Lemma C.20
.

Recall
\bm{R}_{t}:=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with (
Prec-GD
), then

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

Proof.

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}\quad

\left[\text{since }\bm{R}_{t}\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)=\bm{0}\text{ by \lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{BV-perp}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\eta\left(\bm{I}_{d}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right)\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{I}_{d}-\eta\left(\bm{I}_{d}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,\quad

\left[\left\|\bm{I}_{d}-\eta\left(\bm{I}_{d}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{op}\leq 1-\eta,\text{ since }\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\text{ is a rank-}r\text{ projection matrix}\right]

which concludes the proof.
∎

Finally, we have the following linear convergence under (
Prec-GD
) and (
Spectral-init
).

Theorem C.21
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the linear setting, with (
Spectral-init
) and (
Prec-GD
), we choose

\displaystyle\epsilon\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2\sqrt{r^{*}}\kappa}\,,\frac{1}{4}\right\}

and set
\eta\in\left(0,\frac{0.5-2\epsilon}{(1+\epsilon)^{2}}\right)
, then with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{\eta}{2}\right)^{t}\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\,.

Proof.

We prove it by induction.
We suppose the following two inductive hypothesis

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{2}\,,

(49)

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{2}\,.

(50)

Starting from
t=0
, under (
Spectral-init
), with probability at least
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}-\bm{I}_{d}\right)\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\quad

[by
Eq.

28
]

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\|\Delta\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\sqrt{r^{*}}\|\Delta\|_{op}

\left[\text{since }\operatorname{Rank}\left(\Delta\right)=r^{*}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{2}\,.

\left[\text{since }\epsilon\leq 1/2\sqrt{r^{*}}\kappa\right]

Then, by Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\Delta\right)-\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}\leq\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

which implies

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{2}\,.

(51)

Therefore, we verify
Eq.

49
and
Eq.

50
at
t=0
. We assume
Eq.

49
and
Eq.

50
hold at
t=2,3,...
, then by
Lemma

C.19
, with probability at least with probability
1-2C\exp(-\epsilon^{2}N)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{R}_{t+1}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}-\eta\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}^{-1}_{t}\mathcal{U}_{t}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\quad

[by
Lemma

C.20
]

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}(1+\epsilon)^{2}\frac{\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)}\quad

\left[\text{by }\|\bm{\Xi}\|_{op}\leq\epsilon\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta\epsilon\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}(1+\epsilon)^{2}\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\quad

\left[\text{since \lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{inductive-joint-rth-singular-value-lower} and \lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{inductive-loss-hypothesis} hold at }t\right]

\displaystyle=\left(1-(1-2\epsilon)\eta+\eta^{2}(1+\epsilon)^{2}\right)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{R}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.\quad

\left[\text{taking}~\eta\leq\frac{0.5-2\epsilon}{(1+\epsilon)^{2}}\right]

This implies
Eq.

50
at time
t+1
. By consequence, we can obtain
Eq.

49
at time
t+1
again by Weyl’s inequality.
∎

Remark:
The convergence rate is independent of the condition number of
\kappa
. The choice of stepsize
\eta
is upper bounded by
\frac{0.5-2\epsilon}{(1+\epsilon)^{2}}\in(0,0.5)
, which is a decreasing function of
\epsilon
.
Therefore, if the condition number
\kappa
is very large and thus
\epsilon
is chosen as sufficiently small, then
\eta
can reach
0.5
and we still have a fast convergence rate independent of
\kappa
.
This is particularly useful in practical fine-tuning tasks, where the adapted matrix can be highly ill-conditioned when its rank increases. We can empirically observe the ill-conditioned issues in real-world benchmarks, as shown in
Section

G.5
for more discussions.

Appendix D
Proofs for Nonlinear Model

We deliver the proofs for nonlinear models in
Section

4
here.
The problem setting and results for
\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}
are presented in
Section

D.1
.
In
Section

D.2
, we present the proofs of
Theorem

4.2
as well as proofs for smoothed GD.

D.1
Problem Settings and Spectral Initialization

Recall the pre-training model from
Assumption

2.1

\displaystyle f_{\text{pre}}\left(\bm{x}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma\left(\bm{x}^{\!\top}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)^{\!\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}\,,\quad\bm{W}^{\natural}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}\,,

(52)

and the downstream teacher weights from
Assumption

2.2

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}=\bm{W}^{\natural}+\Delta\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}\,,\quad\mbox{with}~\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}:=\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{1}^{\natural},\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{2}^{\natural},\cdots,\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k}^{\natural}\end{bmatrix}\,.

The empirical loss of LoRA fine-tuning is defined as

\begin{split}\widetilde{L}\left(\bm{A}_{t},\bm{B}_{t}\right)&=\frac{1}{2N}\left\|\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\,.\end{split}

Next, we can derive the empirical gradients for
\bm{A}_{t}
and
\bm{B}_{t}
respectively.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{A}}\widetilde{L}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\,,\bm{B}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})\right)\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}_{t})\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}_{t}\right)\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\quad

\displaystyle=-\left[\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}_{t}\right)}_{:=\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}_{t}\right)\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}_{t}\right)}_{:=\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}}\right]\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}

(53)

\displaystyle:=-\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}

where the matrix operator
\bm{J}_{\bm{W}}:\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
is formally defined as (by denoting
\bm{W}_{t}:=\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
)

\displaystyle\bm{J}_{\bm{W}}:\bm{W}\rightarrow\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W})\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}\right)\,.

(54)

Similarly, we can compute

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{B}}\widetilde{L}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\,,\bm{B}_{t}\right)
