\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}_{t})\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=-\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\,.

For full fine-tuning, we consider the following empirical loss function over
\bm{K}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}

\begin{split}{L}\left(\bm{K}\right)&=\frac{1}{2N}\left\|\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{K}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\,.\end{split}

The gradient w.r.t.
\bm{K}
is

\nabla L\left(\bm{K}\right)=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{K}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{K}\right)

Next, we can define the one-step negative gradient of full fine-tuning in the nonlinear case as

\displaystyle\bm{G}^{\natural}

\displaystyle:=-\nabla L\left(\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\left[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)\right]\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}_{\bm{W}^{\natural}}\,.

[by definition of
\bm{J}_{\bm{W}}
in
Eq.

54
]

Additionally, we define

\begin{split}\bm{\Gamma}_{1}^{\natural}&=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)\,,\\
\bm{\Gamma}_{2}^{\natural}&=\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\bm{W}^{\natural}\right)\,.\end{split}

(55)

In this section, we aim to analyze the initial properties of low-rank adapters under (
Spectral-init
) in a nonlinear context. The high-level proof strategy begins with examining the spectral properties of the one-step full gradient matrix,
\bm{G}^{\natural}
. Unlike the linear case, the presence of nonlinearity prevents a direct analysis. To address this, we first establish the concentration of the empirical full gradient, leveraging the fact that the empirical gradient approximates its expectation closely when the sample size is sufficiently large.

Subsequently, we utilize tools from
(
Brutzkus & Globerson, 2017
)
to derive useful properties of the expected gradients. These properties are then transferred back to the empirical gradients through concentration results. Finally, since low-rank adapters under (
Spectral-init
) represent the best
r
-rank approximation of
\bm{G}^{\natural}
, we apply matrix analysis techniques to derive the desired results. Also, the concentration results in this part can serve as an important component for the later convergence analysis.

D.1.1
Computation of Full Population Gradients

First, we can simplify
\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}
and
\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}
which defined in
Eq.

53
to be

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}{\begin{bmatrix}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{1}^{\natural}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,1}\right)&\ldots&\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k}^{\natural}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,k}\right)\end{bmatrix}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}{\begin{bmatrix}\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,1}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,1}\right)&\ldots&\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,k}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,k}\right)\end{bmatrix}}\,,

where
\bm{w}_{t,m}
is the
m
-th column of
\bm{W}_{t}:=\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
and
\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}
is the
m
-th column of
\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}
.

The following two lemmas provide the columnwise expectation of
\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}
and
\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}
respectively.

Lemma D.1
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, for any
\,1\leq m\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\widetilde{\bm{x}}\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\bm{w}_{t,m}+\left(\pi-\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right)\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right]\,,

(56)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{x}\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{w}_{t,m}\,.

(57)

Proof.

First, for any
\,1\leq m\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right)\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\bm{w}_{t,m}}\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\bigg[\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\bm{w}_{t,m}+\left(\pi-\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right)\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right]\,.

[by
Lemma

E.4
]

The proof for
Eq.

57
is the same as that of
Eq.

56
, i.e.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\left[\frac{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\bm{w}_{t,m})\bm{w}_{t,m}+\left(\pi-\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\bm{w}_{t,m})\right)\bm{w}_{t,m}\right]

[by
Lemma

E.4
]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bm{w}_{t,m}\,.

∎

Next, we can obtain the expected full gradients via the following lemma.

Lemma D.2
.

Recall
\bm{W}_{t}:=\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
, suppose
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}
for some constant
\rho\in(0,1)
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting and
Assumption

4.1
, then it holds that

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)+\bm{\Psi}(t)\,,

where
\bm{\Psi}(t)
is defined as

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}(t):=\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)\mathbf{D}_{3}(t)+\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\bigg[\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{2}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)(\bm{I}_{k}-\mathbf{D}_{3}(t))\bigg]\,,

with

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{1}(t):=\operatorname{Diag}\Bigg\{\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{1}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,1}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{1})\right]\,,\ldots\,,\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,k}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{k})\right]\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{2}(t):=\operatorname{Diag}\Bigg\{\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{1}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,1}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{1})\,,\ldots\,,\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,k}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{k})\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{3}(t):=\operatorname{Diag}\left\{\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{1}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{1}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,1}\|_{2}}\,,\ldots\,,\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{k}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,k}\|_{2}}\right\}\,.

(58)

Then we have the following upper bound

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bm{\Psi}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}}{\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}}\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right).

Proof.

We first give some notations here. Let
\bm{w}_{t,m}
be the
m
-th column of
\bm{W}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
,
\bm{w}^{\natural}_{m}
be the
m
-th column of
\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}
,
\Delta_{m}
as the
m
-th of the low-rank shift
\Delta
,
[\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}]_{m}
as the
m
-th column of
\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
.

By
Lemma

D.1
, we can derive
m
-th column of
-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]
for any
m=1,2,\cdots,k
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}\right)\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\bm{w}_{t,m}-\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})-\frac{1}{2\pi}\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\bm{w}_{t,m}-\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left([\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}]_{m}-\Delta_{m}\right)-\frac{1}{2\pi}\underbrace{\left[\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\bm{w}_{t,m}-\theta(\bm{w}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\right]}_{\text{residual part}~{\tt R}}\,.

(59)

Denote

\bm{r}_{t,m}:=[\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}]_{m}-\Delta_{m}\,,

(60)

then we can write

\bm{w}_{t,m}=\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+[\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}]_{m}-\Delta_{m}=\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,

as a relative perturbed time-dependent vector. Next, we take it back to the residual part in
59

\displaystyle{\tt R}=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right](\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m})-\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]\bm{r}_{t,m}

\displaystyle-\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}-\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}

\displaystyle+\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\,.

(61)

Combining
59
and
61
, we can write
-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]
in matrix form as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)+\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)\mathbf{D}_{3}(t)+\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\bigg[\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{2}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)(\bm{I}_{k}-\mathbf{D}_{3}(t))\bigg]\,.

Additionally, we define

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}(t):=

\displaystyle\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)\mathbf{D}_{3}(t)+\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\bigg[\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{2}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)(\bm{I}_{k}-\mathbf{D}_{3}(t))\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)\mathbf{D}_{3}(t)}_{:=\bm{\Psi}_{1}(t)}+\underbrace{\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}(\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{2}(t))}_{:=\bm{\Psi}_{2}(t)}-\underbrace{\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}(\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)(\bm{I}_{k}-\mathbf{D}_{3}(t)))}_{:=\bm{\Psi}_{3}(t)}\,.

(62)

For notational simplicity, we drop time & column index and denote
\bm{w}:=\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}
and
\bm{r}:=\bm{r}_{t,m}
. By condition
\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}
, we have

\|\bm{r}\|_{2}\leq\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\,.

Next, by
Assumption

4.1
, we can obtain

\quad\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\,.

(63)

Part I: Control the Angle
\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})

We denote
\alpha:=\frac{\langle\bm{r}\,,\bm{w}\rangle}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{2}}
and
\beta:=\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{2}}
, then we can derive

\displaystyle\cos\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})=\frac{1+\frac{\langle\bm{r}\,,\bm{w}\rangle}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{2}}}{\sqrt{1+2\frac{\langle\bm{r}\,,\bm{w}\rangle}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{2}}}}=\frac{1+\alpha}{\sqrt{1+2\alpha+\beta}}\,,

which can imply

\displaystyle\sin\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})=

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-\cos^{2}\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})}=\sqrt{\frac{\beta-\alpha^{2}}{1+2\alpha+\beta}}=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\beta-\alpha^{2}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\right)\,.

(64)

By consequence, we can obtain

\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\right)\,.

(65)

Lastly, by the fact that
x-\sin x\leq\frac{x^{3}}{6}
if
x\geq 0
, then we can have

\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})-\sin\theta(\bm{w}+\bm{r}\,,\bm{w})=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}^{3}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}^{3}}\right)\,.

(66)

Part II: Control the Ratio
\left|1-\frac{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}+\bm{r}\|_{2}}\right|

We can compute

\displaystyle\left|1-\frac{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}+\bm{r}\|_{2}}\right|=\left|1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\alpha+\beta}}\right|=\Bigg|1-\Theta\left(1-\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)\Bigg|=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\right)\,.

(67)

where the second equality follows from the first order binomial approximation
\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+x}}=\Theta\left(1-\frac{x}{2}\right)
if
|x|\ll 1
and we have
\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)
by
Eq.

63
.
By consequence, we can have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}+\bm{r}\|_{2}}\leq 1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}\|_{2}}\right)\,.

(68)

Now, we can upper bound
62
in terms of Frobenius norm by triangle inequality, i.e.

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Psi}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{1}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{2}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{3}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

For
\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{1}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{1}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,1}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\right)\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

[by
Eq.

64
and
Eq.

68
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

[by
Eq.

63
]

For
\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{2}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{2}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\|\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)-\mathbf{D}_{2}(t)\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\left(\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]-\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}^{3}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}^{3}}\right)^{2}}

[by
Eq.

66
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\mathcal{O}\left(\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,i}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}^{3}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,i}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}^{3}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}}{\min_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}}\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,i}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\kappa r^{*})^{2}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

[by
Assumption

4.1
and
Eq.

63
]

For
\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{3}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Psi}_{3}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\|\mathbf{D}_{1}(t)(\bm{I}_{k}-\mathbf{D}_{3}(t))\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\left[\left(1-\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\right)\sin\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]\right]^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\left|\left(1-\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}\right)\right|\times\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\sin^{2}\left[\theta(\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}+\bm{r}_{t,m}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m})\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}

[by
Eq.

64
and
Eq.

67
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\sqrt{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}^{2}}{\min_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}

[due to the positivity of
\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}
]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\|_{op}}{\min_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{i}\|_{2}}\max_{1\leq m\leq k}\frac{\|\bm{r}_{t,m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}^{\natural}_{m}\|_{2}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

[by
Assumption

4.1
and
Eq.

63
]

Combine the above upper bounds together, we can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bm{\Psi}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}}{\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}}\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\,,

which completes the proof.
∎

D.1.2
Concentration of Empirical Gradients

In this part, we aim to provide the concentration of empirical gradient
\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}:=\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}-\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
in Frobenius norm. Recall
\bm{W}_{t}:=\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}
and
\bm{w}_{t,m}
is the corresponding
m
-th column of
\bm{W}_{t}
, denote
\widetilde{x}_{i,j}
as the
j
-th element of
\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}
, for notational simplicity, we define each element of
\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}:=\bm{\Gamma}_{1,t}-\bm{\Gamma}_{2,t}
as

\displaystyle c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle:=\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\widetilde{x}_{i,j}\in\mathbb{R}\,,\quad\text{for }1\leq m\leq k\,,1\leq i\leq N\,,1\leq j\leq d\,,

Then, we can write
\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}
in an element-wise way

\displaystyle\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\begin{bmatrix}c^{1}_{t,1}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)&\ldots&c^{1}_{t,k}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)\\
\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
c^{d}_{t,1}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)&\ldots&c^{d}_{t,k}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}\,,

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]\bigg\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right)\right]\right)^{2}\,.

Next, we have the following lemma.

Lemma D.3
.

For
1\leq m\leq k
,
1\leq j\leq d
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, with probability at least
1-2C\operatorname{exp}\left(-N\epsilon^{2}\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
and
\epsilon\in(0,1)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C^{*}K^{2}\epsilon\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\|_{2}\,,

for some absolute constant
C^{*}>0
and
K=\sqrt{8/3}
.

Proof.

Since
\widetilde{x}_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0\,,1)
for any
\,1\leq m\leq k
and
\,1\leq j\leq d
, then we have that
K:=\|\widetilde{x}_{i,j}\|_{\psi_{2}}=\sqrt{8/3}
. By the Orlicz-based definition of subgaussian norm, the subgaussian norm of random variable is identical to its absolute value. Then, for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
, we have the following moment generating function

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{exp}\left(\lambda\left|\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{exp}\left(\lambda\left|\left\langle\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right\rangle\right|\right)\right]\quad

[by Lipschitz continuity of
\sigma
and
\sigma^{\prime}
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{exp}\left((C^{*})^{2}\lambda^{2}\left\|\left|\left\langle\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right\rangle\right|\right\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\right)\right]\,,\quad

[by subgaussian property]

for some constant
C^{*}>0
, which implies

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(C^{*})^{2}\left\|\left|\left\langle\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\,,\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right\rangle\right|\right\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}=(C^{*}K)^{2}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\|_{2}^{2}\,,

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
\|X\|_{\psi_{2}}=Ks
if
X\sim\mathcal{N}(0,s^{2})
. Therefore, by
Vershynin (2018, Lemma 2.7.7)
, this implies
c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)
is sub-exponential with

\displaystyle B_{t,m}:=\|c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right)\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\|\widetilde{x}_{i,j}\|_{\psi_{2}}\left\|\left(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\!\top}\bm{w}_{t,m}^{\natural}\right)\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq C^{*}K^{2}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\|_{2}\,.

(69)

Then, let
\epsilon_{t,m}=C^{*}K^{2}\epsilon\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\|_{2}
for
\epsilon\in(0\,,1)
, we can apply Bernstein’s inequality for sub-exponential variables
Vershynin (2018, Corollary 2.8.3)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right)\right]\right|\geq\epsilon_{t,m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C\operatorname{exp}\left(-N\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon_{t,m}}{B_{t,m}}\,,\frac{\epsilon_{t,m}^{2}}{B_{t,m}^{2}}\right\}\right)\quad

[\text{ for some constant }C>0]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C\operatorname{exp}\left(-N\epsilon^{2}\right)\,.\quad

[by
Eq.

69
and
\epsilon\in(0\,,1)
]

∎

Theorem D.4
.

Suppose
\epsilon\in(0,1)
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, then with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}\left(-N\epsilon^{2}\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]\bigg\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq C^{*}K^{2}\sqrt{d}\epsilon\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\,,

for some absolute constant
C^{*}>0
and
K=\sqrt{8/3}
.

Proof.

By a union bound argument and
Lemma

D.3
, with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}\left(-N\epsilon^{2}\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]\bigg\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{i}\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[c^{j}_{t,m}\left(\widetilde{\bm{x}}\right)\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\epsilon^{2}_{t,m}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{k}(C^{*}K^{2})^{2}\epsilon^{2}\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}-{\bm{w}}_{t,m}^{\natural}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=d(C^{*}K^{2})^{2}\epsilon^{2}\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,,

which implies

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]\bigg\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq C^{*}K^{2}\sqrt{d}\epsilon\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\,,

which finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.5
.

Recall
\bm{G}^{\natural}:=-\nabla L(\bm{W}^{\natural})=\bm{J}_{\bm{W}^{\natural}}
, under
Assumption

4.1
and assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, with (
Spectral-init
), suppose
\epsilon\leq\frac{\rho}{3C^{*}K^{2}\gamma\sqrt{2d}r^{*}\kappa}
for some positive constant
\rho>0
and we set
\gamma=2
,
then with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}(-N\epsilon^{2})
for a universal constant
C>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,.

Proof.

We start with decompose
\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}
into three components, i.e.

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\gamma\bm{G}^{\natural}\right\|_{op}}_{\text{low-rank approximation error}}+\underbrace{\gamma\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right\|_{op}}_{\text{concentration error}}+\underbrace{\left\|\gamma\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]-\Delta\right\|_{op}}_{\text{population error}}\,.

(70)

First, for the population error, we can use similar technique from
Lemma

D.2
, since
\frac{\|\Delta_{m}\|_{2}}{\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{m}^{\natural}\|_{2}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)
by
Assumption

4.1
for
1\leq m\leq k
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]-\frac{1}{2}\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}}{\|\Delta\|_{\rm F}}\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\,.

Next, for the concentration error, following
Theorem

D.4
, we replace
\bm{W}_{t}
with
\bm{W}^{\natural}
and then obtain the following concentration with the probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}(-N\epsilon^{2})
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\rho\|\Delta\|_{\rm F}}{3\sqrt{2}r^{*}\gamma\kappa}\leq\frac{\rho\sqrt{r^{*}}\|\Delta\|_{op}}{3\sqrt{2}r^{*}\gamma\kappa}=\frac{\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{3\sqrt{2r^{*}}\gamma}\,,

(71)

where
\epsilon\leq\frac{\rho}{2C^{*}K^{2}\gamma\sqrt{2d}r^{*}\kappa}
for
\rho>0
.

Lastly, we can upper bound the
(r^{*}+1)
-th singular value of
\bm{G}^{\natural}
(with scale parameter
\gamma
) which acts as the low-rank approximation error. Due to the randomness contained in
\bm{G}^{\natural}
, we decompose the
(r^{*}+1)
-th singular value into two components, i.e.

\displaystyle\gamma\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)\leq\underbrace{\left|\gamma\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)-\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\gamma\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right)\right|}_{\text{concentration error}}+\underbrace{\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\gamma\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right)}_{\text{population error}}\,.

First, for the concentration error, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left|\gamma\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)-\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\gamma\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right\|_{op}\quad

\left[\text{by Weyl's inequality}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{3\sqrt{2r^{*}}}\,.\quad

[by
Eq.

71
]

Second, we can obtain the population error as

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right)=

\displaystyle\left|\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right)-\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\Delta\right)\right|

[since
\operatorname{Rank}(\Delta)=r^{*}
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]-\frac{1}{2}\Delta\right\|_{op}

\left[\text{by Weyl's inequality}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]-\frac{1}{2}\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\|\Delta\|_{\rm F}\,.

Now we can have

\displaystyle\gamma\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)\leq\frac{\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{3\sqrt{2r^{*}}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\|\Delta\|_{\rm F}}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{r^{*}}}\right)\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\,.

(72)

Therefore, combine everything together, recall
Eq.

70
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\lambda_{r^{*}+1}\left(\bm{G}^{\natural}\right)+\gamma\left\|\bm{G}^{\natural}-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]\right\|_{\rm F}+\gamma\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{G}^{\natural}\right]-\frac{1}{\gamma}\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{r^{*}}}\right)\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}+\frac{\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{3\sqrt{2r^{*}}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{r^{*}}}\right)\rho\lambda_{r^{*}}^{*}\leq\frac{\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}{\sqrt{2r^{*}}}\,.

(73)

Since we work in the exact-rank case
\operatorname{Rank}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)\leq r=r^{*}
with
\operatorname{Rank}(\Delta)=r^{*}
, then
\operatorname{Rank}(\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta)\leq 2r^{*}
, this can imply

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2r^{*}}\left\|\bm{A}_{0}\bm{B}_{0}-\Delta\right\|_{op}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,,

which completes the proof.
∎

D.2
Preconditioned Gradient Descent under Spectral Initialization

Recall the loss of LoRA fine-tuning:

\displaystyle\widetilde{L}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\,,\bm{B}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2N}\left\|\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\left(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}^{2}\,.

Then, we employ the following preconditioned gradient updates for LoRA fine-tuning

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}+\eta\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{-1}\,,

(74)

and

\displaystyle\bm{B}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}_{t}+\eta\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\,.

(75)

Similar to the linear case, we define the following notations

•

SVD of product matrix
\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}:=\mathcal{U}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}\mathcal{V}_{t}^{\!\top}
, where
\mathcal{U}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
,
\mathcal{S}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r^{*}\times r}
, and
\mathcal{V}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times r}
.

•

The left singular matrix of
\bm{A}_{t}
as
\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
.

•

The right singular matrix of
\bm{B}_{t}
as
\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times r}
.

Lemma D.6
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, we update
\bm{A}_{t}
and
\bm{B}_{t}
via
Eq.

74
and
Eq.

75
under spectral initialization (
Spectral-init
), then we have the following recursion

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta

\displaystyle=(1-\eta)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\eta/2)\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\eta/2)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\,,

(76)

and

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}_{t}:=\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\,.

Then, by choosing
\eta\in(0\,,1)
, we have the associated upper bound in Frobenius norm

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

(77)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+(1-\eta/2)\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+2\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

(78)

Proof.

By the preconditioned update in
Eq.

74
and
Eq.

75
, we can construct

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}-\eta\bm{A}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2\bm{A}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)+\eta\bm{A}_{t}\left(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)+\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\,,

[by pseudo inverse theorem and
Jia et al. (2024, Lemma 14)
]

from our choice on
\bm{\Xi}_{t}=\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)
.
We can continue to expand

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta

\displaystyle=\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta/2\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\eta/2)\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\eta/2)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\eta\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}+\eta^{2}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\,.

Based on the above formulation, suppose
\eta\in\left(0\,,1\right)
, we can derive the following upper bound by triangle inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

(79)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|(1-\eta)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|(1-\eta/2)\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|(1-\eta/2)\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+(1-\eta/2)\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+2\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

which proves the claim.
∎

In order to derive the convergence rate of
\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}
in the above terms, we need to provide the estimation of the following four terms

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,\quad\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

which are important elements in
Eq.

77
. We firstly prove the upper bound for
\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\|_{\rm F}
and
\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}
since they are relatively straightforward. After that, we will handle with the remaining three terms which are the most technical part.
All of these three terms rely on the condition
\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}
and we will prove it by induction finally in
Theorem

D.10
.

Lemma D.7
.

For a positive constant
\rho\in(0,1)
, suppose
\epsilon\leq\frac{\rho}{3C^{*}K^{2}\gamma\sqrt{2d}r^{*}\kappa}
with
\gamma=2
, assume
\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}
, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting and
Assumption

4.1
, then with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}\left(-\epsilon^{2}N\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)+C^{*}K^{2}\sqrt{d}\epsilon\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

Proof.

Recall
\bm{\Xi}_{t}:=\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)
from
Lemma

D.6
, then with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}\left(-\epsilon^{2}N\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]-\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\|\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)-\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]\right\|_{\rm F}}_{\text{population error}}+\underbrace{\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]-\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}}_{\text{concentration error}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|\bm{\Psi}(t)\right\|_{\rm F}}{\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}+\left\|\mathbb{E}_{\widetilde{\bm{x}}}\left[\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right]-\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}\quad

[by
Lemma

D.2
]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)+C^{*}K^{2}\sqrt{d}\epsilon\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,,\quad

[by
Lemma

D.2
and
Theorem

D.4
]

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma D.8
.

Under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting, suppose
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}
for a positive constant
\rho>0
, with probability at least
1-2C\operatorname{exp}\left(-\epsilon^{2}N\right)
for some constants
C>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\epsilon)^{2}\frac{\rho}{1-\rho}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

Proof.

First, with probability at least
1-2C\operatorname{exp}\left(-\epsilon^{2}N\right)
for some constants
C>0
, we can derive

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\bigg(\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})\right)-\sigma\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}\widetilde{\bm{W}}^{\natural}\right)\bigg)\odot\sigma^{\prime}\left(\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{W}^{\natural}+\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t})\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\lambda_{r}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|\frac{1}{N}\widetilde{\bm{X}}^{\!\top}\widetilde{\bm{X}}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\lambda_{r}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)}\quad

[by Lipschitz continuity of
\sigma\,,\sigma^{\prime}
]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{N}\lambda^{2}_{1}(\widetilde{\bm{X}})\right)^{2}\frac{\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\lambda_{r}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\epsilon)^{2}\frac{\rho}{1-\rho}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,,\quad

[by concentration of operator norm]

where the last equality follows from
r=r^{*}
and

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r^{*}}(\Delta)-\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\geq(1-\rho)\lambda_{r^{*}}(\Delta)\,.

∎

With
Lemma

D.7
and
Lemma

D.8
, now we can prove for the other three terms.

Lemma D.9
.

Suppose
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}
with a positive constant
\rho\in[0\,,1/4]
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{1-8\rho^{2}}}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

and

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

Proof.

First, we recall

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{t}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\end{bmatrix}\,,\quad\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\\
-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\end{bmatrix}\,,

and define a preconditioned operator
\mathcal{P}
and symmetrized downstream feature shift matrix
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}(\bm{Z}_{t}):=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\end{bmatrix}\,,\quad\mathcal{P}(\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}):=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\\
-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\end{bmatrix}\,,\quad\hat{\bm{\Delta}}:=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}_{d\times d}&\Delta\\
\Delta^{\!\top}&\bm{0}_{k\times k}\end{bmatrix}\,.

Next, we observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)-\hat{\bm{\Delta}}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{0}_{d\times d}&\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\\
\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right)^{\!\top}&\bm{0}_{k\times k}\end{bmatrix}\,,

leading to

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)-\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\right\|_{op}=\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{op}\,,\quad\left\|\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)-\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\right\|_{\rm F}=\sqrt{2}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

Based on the compact SVD of
\Delta
in
Eq.

1
, we can write out the eigendecomposition of
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
as

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\Delta}}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Phi}&\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{S}^{*}&\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times r^{*}}\\
\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times r^{*}}&-\bm{S}^{*}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Phi}&\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\end{bmatrix}^{\!\top}=\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\,,\quad\text{where }\bm{\Phi}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}\\
\bm{V}\end{bmatrix}\,,\underline{\bm{\Phi}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}\\
-\bm{V}\end{bmatrix}\,.

(80)

Notice that we can also obtain the SVD of
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
as

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\Delta}}=\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}=\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Phi}&\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\end{bmatrix}}_{:=\widehat{\bm{U}}}\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{S}^{*}&\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times r^{*}}\\
\bm{0}_{r^{*}\times r^{*}}&\bm{S}^{*}\end{bmatrix}}_{\widehat{\bm{S}}}\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\Phi}&-\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\end{bmatrix}^{\!\top}}_{:=\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}}\,.

(81)

Notice that
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
is a low-rank matrix with rank-
2r^{*}
because of
\operatorname{Rank}\left(\Delta\right)=r^{*}
. If
\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)
recovers
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
, this indicates that the top-
2r^{*}
subspace of
\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)
will align to
\hat{\bm{\Delta}}
perfectly.
Next, we can derive the projection matrix for the top-
2r^{*}
subspace of
\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)
. First, we have

\displaystyle\bm{Z}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\bm{Z}_{t})=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}\end{bmatrix}\,,

which can imply

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bm{Z}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\bm{Z}_{t})\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{A}_{t}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t})^{-1}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}(\bm{B}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top})^{-1}\bm{B}_{t}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{B}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\\
\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{B}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\frac{1}{2}\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}\,.

Similarly, we can derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t})\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{A}_{t}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&-\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\\
-\bm{B}_{t}^{\!\top}\bm{A}_{t}^{\!\top}&\bm{B}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\frac{1}{2}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\,.

Additionally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}){\bm{Z}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}=\bm{0}_{(d+k)\times(d+k)}\,,\quad\frac{1}{2}{\bm{Z}}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}({\bm{Z}}_{t})\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}=\bm{0}_{(d+k)\times(d+k)}\,.

Base on the above identity, we can obtain that the subspace of
{\bm{Z}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}
is orthogonal to the subspace of
\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}
. Since
\operatorname{Rank}\left({\bm{Z}}_{t}{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\leq r
and
\operatorname{Rank}\left(\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\leq r
, then we have that
\operatorname{Rank}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\leq 2r^{*}
since
r=r^{*}
. Therefore, we can construct a valid projection matrix

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t}:=\bm{Z}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\bm{Z}_{t})+\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\mathcal{P}^{\!\top}(\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t})\,,

(82)

which satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{P}_{t}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{I}_{d+k}-\mathbf{P}_{t}\right)\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)=\bm{0}_{(d+k)\times(d+k)}\,.

(83)

Also, we can verify that
\mathbf{P}_{t}
is symmetric and
\mathbf{P}_{t}\mathbf{P}_{t}=\mathbf{P}_{t}
.
Therefore we can conclude that
\mathbf{P}_{t}
is the projection matrix which maps matrices or vectors to the top-
2r
subspace of
\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)
. For notational simplicity, here we fix the timestamp
t
and denote

\displaystyle\bm{F}:=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left(\bm{Z}_{t}\bm{Z}_{t}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}\underline{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{\!\top}\right)\,,

which means

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|_{\rm F}=\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,.

(84)

Next, we define
\mathbf{P}_{t}:=\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{(d+k)\times(d+k)}
with

\displaystyle\bm{L}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}&\bm{0}_{d\times r}\\
\bm{0}_{k\times r}&\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{(d+k)\times 2r}\,,

and
\left(\bm{I}_{d+k}-\mathbf{P}_{t}\right)=\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}
where

\displaystyle\bm{L}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t},\perp}&\bm{0}_{d\times(k-r)}\\
\bm{0}_{k\times(d-r)}&\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t},\perp}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{(d+k)\times(d+k-2r)}\,,

then we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\\
\bm{L}^{\!\top}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\left(\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\bm{L}&\bm{L}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\bm{F}\bm{L}-\bm{L}^{\!\top}\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{L}&-\bm{L}^{\!\top}\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\\
-\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{L}&\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

[by
Eq.

83
]

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\bm{F}\bm{L}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,.

(85)

Since
\bm{I}_{d+k}-\mathbf{P}_{t}=\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}
, then we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

(86)

Next, by
Eq.

85
, we have
\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}
, leading to

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}\,.

(87)

The technical part is to lower bound
\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}
and
\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}
, we will rely on the following decomposition which based on
Eq.

81
, i.e.

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)^{\!\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)^{\!\top}\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\left(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\right)\,.

Notice that
\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}
and
\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}
are two positive semi-definite matrices, then by lower bound of trace of product of positive semi-definite matrices, using Weyl inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)\left\|\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\times\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\left\|\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\times\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,

where the last equality follows from

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\times\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

Next, we can derive

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}+\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

[by
Eq.

80
]

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\langle\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\,,\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\rangle\,,

and

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}+\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

[by
Eq.

80
]

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\langle\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\,,\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\rangle\,.

Also, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}=\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\left(\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}-\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\right)\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

[by
Eq.

80
]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}-2\left\langle\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\,,\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\rangle\,.

Notice that the matrix inner product term is the inner product of two positive semi-definite matrices, then by trace inequality for positive semi-definite matrices, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\,,\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\operatorname{tr}\left(\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}\bm{S}^{*}\underline{\bm{\Phi}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\left(\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\bm{\Phi}\bm{S}^{*}\bm{\Phi}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right)\right)\geq 0\,.

Then, we can claim

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,,\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,.

(88)

Next, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\times\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\times\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\min\left\{\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\,,\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\widehat{\bm{S}}\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}_{\perp}\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 2\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\min\left\{\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\,,\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\right\}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}\,.

[by
Eq.

88
]

Then, combining the above inequality and
Eq.

87
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}}{2\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\min\left\{\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\,,\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\right\}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}}{2\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\min\left\{\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\,,\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\right\}}\,.

Next, we will focus on the lower bound of
\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)
and
\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)
. Due to symmetry, the technique is identical to each other, so here we only prove for
\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)
. First,
\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)=\lambda^{2}_{2r^{*}}\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)
since
\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}
is symmetric. Next, we have

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}_{2r^{*}}\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)

\displaystyle=1-\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right\|^{2}_{op}\,,

where
\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right\|_{op}
can be upper bounded by Wedin’s
\sin(\Theta)
theorem, here we use a variant from in
Chen et al. (2021b, Theorem 2.9)
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|_{op}}{\lambda^{*}_{2r^{*}}\left(\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right)}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|_{\rm F}}{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}\leq 2\sqrt{2}\rho\,,

\left[\text{by }\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|_{\rm F}\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\right]

which implies

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}_{2r^{*}}\left(\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-8\rho^{2}\,.

(89)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\rho^{2}(\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}})^{2}=\rho^{2}\lambda^{2}_{2r^{*}}(\hat{\bm{\Delta}})

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\quad

[by
Eq.

85
]

\displaystyle\geq\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\min\left\{\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{U}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{U}}\right)\,,\lambda_{2r^{*}}\left(\widehat{\bm{V}}^{\!\top}\bm{L}\bm{L}^{\!\top}\widehat{\bm{V}}\right)\right\}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}\,,\quad

[by
Eq.

89
and
\rho\leq 1/4
]

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|_{\rm F}}{\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}}\leq 2\rho^{2}\,.

Finally, combining
Eq.

86
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\rho^{2}}{1-8\rho^{2}}\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,.

Notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}_{\perp}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\bm{L}^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\bm{L}_{\perp}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{P}_{t}\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\left(\bm{I}_{d+k}-\mathbf{P}_{t}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d+k}-\mathbf{P}_{t}\right)\hat{\bm{\Delta}}\mathbf{P}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,,

then by the decomposition in
Eq.

85
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\Delta\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\Delta\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{F}-\frac{\hat{\bm{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{2}}\right\|^{2}_{\rm F}=\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\|^{2}_{\rm F}\,,

which completes the proof.
∎

Based on the above estimation, we are ready to deliver the linear convergence rate of
\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}
.

Theorem D.10
.

Suppose
\epsilon\leq\frac{\rho}{3C^{*}K^{2}\gamma\sqrt{2d}r^{*}\kappa}
for a positive constant
\rho\leq\frac{1}{20}
, we take
\gamma=2
for (
Spectral-init
), set
\eta\in\left(c_{\eta}\,,1\right)
where
c_{\eta}>0
is a small constant, under assumptions in
Section

2.1
for the nonlinear setting and
Assumption

4.1
, then with probability at least
1-2Cdk\operatorname{exp}\left(-\epsilon^{2}N\right)
for a universal constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta}{4}\right)^{t}\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,.

Proof.

We prove it by induction.
The following hypothesis holds at
t=0
by
Lemma

D.5
, i.e.

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\rho\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,.

We suppose it also holds for at time
t
, then the conditions of
Lemma

D.7
,
Lemma

D.8
, and
Lemma

D.9
are fulfilled. By consequence, we can show that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r^{*}}\left(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq(1-\rho)\lambda^{*}_{r^{*}}\,.

[by Weyl’s inequality]

Next, by
Eq.

77
from
Lemma

D.6
, under initial conditions from
Lemma

D.5
, for time
t+1
, we can derive

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{A}_{t+1}\bm{B}_{t+1}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+(1-\eta/2)\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}+\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\bm{I}_{d}-\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}\bm{U}_{\bm{A}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)(\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta)\left(\bm{I}_{k}-\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}\bm{V}_{\bm{B}_{t}}^{\!\top}\right)\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+2\eta\left\|\bm{\Xi}_{t}\right\|_{\rm F}+\eta^{2}\left\|\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\mathcal{V}_{t}\mathcal{S}_{t}^{-1}\mathcal{U}^{\!\top}_{t}\bm{J}_{\bm{W}_{t}}\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\eta)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle+\left(1-\eta/2+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{1-8\rho^{2}}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

[by
Lemma

D.9
]

\displaystyle+2\eta\left(\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\kappa r^{*}}\right)+C^{*}K^{2}\sqrt{d}\epsilon\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

[by
Lemma

D.7
]

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}(1+\epsilon)^{2}\frac{\rho}{1-\rho}\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}

[by
Lemma

D.8
]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2-3\eta/2+\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{1-8\rho^{2}}}\right)\left\|\bm{A}_{t}\bm{B}_{t}-\Delta\right\|_{\rm F}
